# Supporting types

Generated by `bun run docs:protocol`. Edit source schemas, then regenerate.

JSON Schema validates structure. `x-ace-constraint` names additional semantic rules that clients must enforce; Zod remains the full validator.

## AccountAssignment

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountAssignment.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| instanceId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| role | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":64} |
| estimatedLoad | yes | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "estimatedLoad": 6,
  "role": "example"
}
```

## AccountAvailability

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountAvailability.json), input validation.

Type: ["available","near_limit","exhausted","logged_out","unknown"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"logged_out"
```

## AccountDirectory

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountDirectory.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Absolute Unix or drive-qualified Windows directory.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"/example/home"
```

## AccountEnvKey

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountEnvKey.json), input validation.

Type: ["CODEX_HOME","CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR","XDG_DATA_HOME","XDG_CONFIG_HOME","XDG_STATE_HOME","XDG_CACHE_HOME","CURSOR_DATA_DIR","PI_CODING_AGENT_DIR"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json

```

## AccountQuota

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountQuota.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| auth | yes | ["logged_in","logged_out","unknown"] |  |
| cursorSdkAuth | no | [CursorSdkAuth.json](schema/CursorSdkAuth.json) |  |
| observedAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| windows | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"},"maxProperties":32,"x-ace-constraint":"At most 32 quota windows."} |
| blockers | no | object | {"default":{},"properties":{"overflow":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"limitError":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"}}} |
| usage | yes | object | {"properties":{"inputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"outputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"costUsd":{"type":"number","minimum":0}}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "auth": "logged_out",
  "observedAt": 6,
  "usage": {
    "inputTokens": 2
  },
  "windows": {}
}
```

## AccountSummary

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountSummary.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| implicit | no | boolean |  |
| isDefault | no | boolean |  |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| isolation | no | `"unsupported"` |  |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| quota | yes | [AccountQuota.json](schema/AccountQuota.json) |  |
| availability | yes | [AccountAvailability.json](schema/AccountAvailability.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "availability": "exhausted",
  "id": "example",
  "installationVersion": "example",
  "label": "example",
  "provider": "acp",
  "quota": {
    "auth": "logged_in",
    "blockers": {
      "limitError": {
        "resetsAt": 9,
        "usedPercent": 5
      },
      "overflow": true
    },
    "cursorSdkAuth": {
      "source": "sdk-store",
      "status": "logged-out"
    },
    "observedAt": 9,
    "usage": {},
    "windows": {}
  }
}
```

## AcpSessionSupport

[JSON Schema](schema/AcpSessionSupport.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| capabilities | yes | object | {"properties":{"resume":{"type":"boolean"},"imageInput":{"type":"boolean"},"planMode":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["resume","imageInput","planMode"]} |
| mcp | yes | ["http","stdio","unavailable"] |  |
| modelSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| modeSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| subagentSessions | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| coverage | yes | ["source_profile","generic","legacy"] |  |
| visibility | yes | `"limited"` |  |
| raw | yes | object | {"properties":{"json":{"type":"string","maxLength":2048},"truncated":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["json","truncated"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "capabilities": {
    "imageInput": true,
    "planMode": false,
    "resume": true
  },
  "coverage": "legacy",
  "mcp": "http",
  "modeSelection": false,
  "modelSelection": false,
  "raw": {
    "json": "example",
    "truncated": false
  },
  "visibility": "limited"
}
```

## Agent

[JSON Schema](schema/Agent.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| childThreadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| parentId | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AgentId.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| origin | yes | [AgentOrigin.json](schema/AgentOrigin.json) |  |
| lineage | no | [ThreadLineage.json](schema/ThreadLineage.json) |  |
| native | yes | [NativeRef.json](schema/NativeRef.json) |  |
| fidelity | yes | [AgentFidelity.json](schema/AgentFidelity.json) |  |
| spawnedBy | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ItemId.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| name | no | string |  |
| role | no | string |  |
| model | no | string |  |
| cwd | yes | string |  |
| status | yes | [AgentStatus.json](schema/AgentStatus.json) |  |
| background | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| endedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "createdAt": 4,
  "cwd": "example",
  "endedAt": 8,
  "fidelity": "full",
  "id": "example",
  "lineage": {
    "lossy": true,
    "mode": "native",
    "parentAgentId": "example",
    "parentThreadId": "example",
    "point": {
      "itemId": "example",
      "type": "item"
    }
  },
  "native": {
    "aliases": [],
    "provider": "pi"
  },
  "origin": "provider_subagent",
  "parentId": "example",
  "role": "example",
  "status": {
    "activity": "thinking",
    "state": "working"
  },
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"delegate_task"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$"} |
| task | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":16384} |
| role | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":1024} |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| permissionMode | no | [PermissionMode.json](schema/PermissionMode.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| accountId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| options | no | [AgentLaunchOptions.json](schema/AgentLaunchOptions.json) |  |
| wait | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| estimatedLoad | no | number | {"default":0,"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "accountId": "example",
  "instanceId": "example",
  "op": "delegate_task",
  "provider": "pi",
  "requestId": "Kv1QJc2BAQE",
  "role": "example",
  "task": "example"
}
```

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

[JSON Schema](schema/ContextErrorCode.json), input validation.

```json
"invalid_request"
```

| type | yes | `"hello"` |  |
| channel | no | ["files","devices","browser","screen"] |  |
| protocolVersion | yes | `1` |  |
| deviceId | yes | [DeviceId.json](schema/DeviceId.json) |  |
| token | no | string |  |
| ticket | no | string |  |

```json
  "channel": "browser",
  "deviceId": "example",
  "protocolVersion": 1,
  "token": "example",
  "type": "hello"
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"subscribe"` |  |
| subscriptionId | yes | string | {"minLength":1} |
| scope | yes | [SubscriptionScope.json](schema/SubscriptionScope.json) |  |
| paced | no | boolean |  |
| afterSeq | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |

```json
  "afterSeq": 5,
  "paced": false,

    "kind": "threads"
  },
  "subscriptionId": "example",
  "type": "subscribe"
}
```

Semantic rule: seq must equal view.seq.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| subscriptionId | yes | string |  |
| seq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| view | yes | [SnapshotView.json](schema/SnapshotView.json) |  |

```json
  "subscriptionId": "example",

    "agents": {},
    "backgroundTasks": {},
    "interactions": {},
    "itemOrder": [],
    "itemSeqs": {},
    "items": {},
    "kind": "thread",
    "runs": {},

    "thread": {
      "acpSupport": {
        "capabilities": {
          "imageInput": true,
          "planMode": false,
          "resume": false
        },
        "coverage": "legacy",
        "mcp": "stdio",
        "modeSelection": false,
        "modelSelection": true,
        "raw": {
          "json": "example",
          "truncated": true
        },
        "subagentSessions": true,
        "visibility": "limited"
      },
      "activityAt": 4,
      "archivedAt": 7,
      "backend": "cursor-sdk",
      "createdAt": 5,
      "deletedAt": 0,
      "id": "example",
      "lineage": {
        "lossy": false,
        "mode": "native",
        "parentAgentId": "example",
        "parentThreadId": "example",
        "point": {
          "itemId": "example",
          "type": "item"
        }
      },
      "pinned": false,
      "provider": "claude",
      "rootAgentId": "example",
      "status": {
        "state": "done"
      },
      "switch": {
        "at": 8,
        "lossy": true,
        "recommendation": "delegate_task",
        "selection": {
          "provider": "codex"
        },
        "state": "failed"
      },
      "title": "example",
      "updatedAt": 1,
      "workspaceId": "example"
    },
    "usage": {}
  }
}
```

### events

Semantic rule: throughSeq >= afterSeq; events are ordered with (firstSeq ?? seq) > previous seq and seq <= throughSeq.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"events"` |  |
| subscriptionId | yes | string |  |
| afterSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| events | yes | array | {"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DeliveryEvent.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "afterSeq": 1,
  "events": [],
  "subscriptionId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 8,
  "type": "events"
}
```

### progress

Semantic rule: throughSeq must be >= afterSeq.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"progress"` |  |
| subscriptionId | yes | string |  |
| afterSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |

Example:

```json
{
  "afterSeq": 7,
  "subscriptionId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 7,
  "type": "progress"
}
```

## DeviceState

[JSON Schema](schema/DeviceState.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| device | yes | [AppDevice.json](schema/AppDevice.json) |  |
| enabled | yes | boolean |  |
| approved | yes | boolean |  |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| lifecycle | yes | ["idle","starting","live","stopping","failed"] |  |
| streamId | no | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |
| controller | yes | ["none","human","agent"] |  |
| holder | no | object | {"properties":{"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"agentId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AgentId.json"}},"required":["threadId","agentId"]} |
| leaseExpiresAt | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| error | no | [DeviceFailure.json](schema/DeviceFailure.json) |  |

```json
  "approved": false,
  "controller": "none",
  "device": {
    "id": "ios:40499DA270aB426cCdE-57857dA5AfCFA568",
    "name": "example",
    "platform": "android",
    "state": "booted"

  "error": {

    "hint": "example",
    "message": "example"

    "threadId": "example"
  },
  "lifecycle": "starting",
  "streamId": "XelqGqAQFAwhoFr1j9IemHttRADQynTN3PmFy45Lo"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/DiagnosticsHealth.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| at | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| eventLoop | yes | object | {"properties":{"meanMs":{"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]},"p99Ms":{"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]},"maxMs":{"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]}},"required":["meanMs","p99Ms","maxMs"]} |
| memory | yes | object | {"properties":{"rssBytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"heapUsedBytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"heapTotalBytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["rssBytes","heapUsedBytes","heapTotalBytes"]} |
| openHandles | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| sqlite | yes | object | {"properties":{"pageBytes":{"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]},"walBytes":{"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]}},"required":["pageBytes","walBytes"]} |
| activeSessions | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| queues | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"maxProperties":64,"x-ace-constraint":"At most 64 queue entries."} |
| logs | yes | object | {"properties":{"dropped":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"failed":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"queued":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"directory":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096}},"required":["dropped","failed","queued"]} |

Example:

```json
  "activeSessions": null,
  "at": 6,
  "eventLoop": {
    "maxMs": null,
    "meanMs": null,

  },
  "logs": {

    "failed": 9,
    "queued": 9
  },

    "heapTotalBytes": 0,
    "heapUsedBytes": 6,
    "rssBytes": 0
  },
  "openHandles": 0,
  "queues": {},
  "sqlite": {
    "pageBytes": null,
    "walBytes": 7
  }
}
```

## ItemsWindowRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsWindowRequest.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one target; before + after + target is at most 200 items.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"items.window"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| aroundSeq | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| turnOrdinal | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| before | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |
| after | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": 6,
  "aroundSeq": 4,
  "before": 5,
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "items.window"
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| sessionId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| type | yes | `"mcp.notify"` |  |
| notice | yes | [McpNoticeInput.json](schema/McpNoticeInput.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "agentId": "example",
  "notice": {
    "level": "warning",
    "text": "example"
  },
  "sessionId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "mcp.notify"
}
```

| lossy | yes | `true` |  |
| policy | yes | `"recent-complete-items"` |  |
| excerpts | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"citation":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/HandoffCitation.json"},"text":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}},"required":["citation","text"]}} |

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| enabled | no | boolean |  |
| defaultModel | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$","x-ace-constraint":"The pseudo model ID default is not accepted; choose a concrete model ID."},{"type":"null"}]} |
| binaryPath | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096,"pattern":"^(?:\\/\|[A-Za-z]:[\\\\/]).*$","x-ace-constraint":"Absolute executable path with no control bytes."} |
| customModels | no | array | {"maxItems":128,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"displayName":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"\\S"}},"required":["id","displayName"]},"x-ace-constraint":"Custom model IDs must be unique."} |
| hiddenModels | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| shownModels | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| hiddenGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| shownGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| favourites | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |

## ProviderInstance

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderInstance.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Native instances use native account IDs. ACP instances require matching agent, installation and instance references, id equal to instanceId, default_cli home strategy and an empty selector environment.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| implicit | no | `true` |  |
| managed | no | `true` |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| homeDir | yes | [AccountDirectory.json](schema/AccountDirectory.json) |  |
| env | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountEnvKey.json"},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountDirectory.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "env": {
    "CODEX_HOME": "/example/codex"
  },
  "homeDir": "/example/codex",
  "id": "codex-default",
  "label": "Codex",
  "provider": "codex"
}
```

| type | yes | `"registry.install-cancel"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| intentId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |

Example:

```json

  "intentId": "example",
  "requestId": "example",
  "type": "registry.install-cancel"
}
```

## RegistryResult

[JSON Schema](schema/RegistryResult.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"registry.result"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| result | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"object","properties":{"ok":{"type":"boolean","const":false},"reason":{"type":"string","maxLength":512}},"required":["ok","reason"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"ok":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"agents":{"maxItems":50,"type":"array","items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/RegistryAgent.json"}},"installations":{"maxItems":512,"type":"array","items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/RegistryInstallation.json"}},"stale":{"type":"boolean"},"refreshing":{"type":"boolean"},"activeInstall":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/RegistryInstallProgress.json"},"error":{"type":"string","enum":["refresh_failed","cache_failed"]},"source":{"type":"string","maxLength":4096},"digest":{"type":"string","pattern":"^[a-f0-9]{64}$"},"fetchedAt":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"schemaVersion":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"release":{"type":"string","maxLength":256},"nextOffset":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["ok","agents","installations","stale","refreshing","source"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"ok":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"plan":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/RegistryInstallPlan.json"}},"required":["ok","plan"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"ok":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"installation":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/RegistryInstallation.json"},"durability":{"type":"string","const":"uncertain"}},"required":["ok","installation"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"ok":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"cancelled":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["ok","cancelled"]}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "requestId": "example",
  "result": {
    "agents": [],
    "installations": [],
    "ok": true,
    "refreshing": false,
    "source": "example",
    "stale": true
  },
  "type": "registry.result"
}
```

## ReleaseManifest

[JSON Schema](schema/ReleaseManifest.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | [ReleaseVersion.json](schema/ReleaseVersion.json) |  |
| channel | yes | ["stable","preview"] |  |
| target | yes | [ReleaseTarget.json](schema/ReleaseTarget.json) |  |
| archive | yes | string | {"maxLength":160,"pattern":"^ace-[a-z0-9.-]+\\.tar\\.gz$"} |
| bytes | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":268435456} |
| sha256 | yes | string | {"pattern":"^[a-f0-9]{64}$"} |

```json
  "archive": "ace-jv.tar.gz",

  "channel": "stable",
  "sha256": "02e347a3dc284c287ff2c73acae09aa47137a4421475cf4fa9a92b2406daaa33",
  "target": "linux-arm64",
  "version": "8776916588.40024312.20"
}
```

## ReviewAnchor

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewAnchor.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Fingerprint lines count equals end - start + 1.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| position | yes | [ReviewPosition.json](schema/ReviewPosition.json) |  |
| revision | yes | [ReviewRevision.json](schema/ReviewRevision.json) |  |
| fingerprint | yes | [ReviewFingerprint.json](schema/ReviewFingerprint.json) |  |
| state | yes | ["active","outdated","addressed-pending-review"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "fingerprint": {
    "after": [],
    "before": [],
    "lines": [
      "example"
    ]
  },
  "position": {
    "end": 1,
    "file": "example.ts",
    "side": "new",
    "start": 1
  },
  "revision": {
    "kind": "working-tree"
  },
  "state": "active"
}
```

## ReviewFingerprint

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewFingerprint.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Total UTF-16 length of before, lines and after is <= 8192.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| before | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| lines | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":100,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| after | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| noFinalNewline | no | `true` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": [],
  "before": [],
  "lines": [
    "example"
  ]
}
```

| end | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":10000000} |

```json
  "file": "example.ts",
  "side": "new",
  "start": 1
}
```

| endedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| error | no | [AgentError.json](schema/AgentError.json) |  |

Example:

```json

  "agentId": "example",
  "id": "example",
  "startedAt": 4,
  "state": "interrupted",
  "threadId": "example",
  "trigger": "limit_resume"
}
```

## ScreenAction

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenAction.json), input validation.

### click

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"click"` |  |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| button | no | ["left","right"] | {"default":"left"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "kind": "click",
  "x": 2,
  "y": 4
}
```

### type

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"type"` |  |
| text | yes | string | {"maxLength":4096} |

Example:

```json
{
  "kind": "type",
  "text": "example"
}
```

### key

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"key"` |  |
| keyCode | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":127} |
| modifiers | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":4,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["command","shift","option","control"]}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "keyCode": 2,
  "kind": "key",
  "modifiers": []
}
```

### scroll

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"scroll"` |  |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| deltaX | yes | integer | {"minimum":-1000,"maximum":1000} |
| deltaY | yes | integer | {"minimum":-1000,"maximum":1000} |

Example:

```json
{
  "deltaX": 9,
  "deltaY": 3,
  "kind": "scroll",
  "x": 2,
  "y": 2
}
```

## ScreenCapabilities

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenCapabilities.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `2` |  |
| background | no | boolean |  |
| maxSessions | no | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":8} |
| platform | yes | ["macos","windows","linux-x11","linux-wayland"] |  |
| capture | yes | object | {"properties":{"windows":{"type":"boolean"},"displays":{"type":"boolean"},"changeDriven":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["windows","displays","changeDriven"]} |
| input | yes | object | {"properties":{"pointer":{"type":"boolean"},"keyboard":{"type":"boolean"},"scroll":{"type":"boolean"},"text":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["pointer","keyboard","scroll","text"]} |
| uiTree | yes | boolean |  |
| semanticActions | yes | array | {"maxItems":8,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["press","focus","setValue","scroll","expand","select","performSecondaryAction","selectText"]}} |
| codecs | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["jpeg","h264"]}} |
| permissions | yes | object | {"properties":{"screen":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]},"input":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]}},"required":["screen","input"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "capture": {
    "changeDriven": false,
    "displays": true,
    "windows": true
  },
  "codecs": [
    "h264"
  ],
  "input": {
    "keyboard": true,
    "pointer": false,
    "scroll": true,
    "text": false
  },
  "permissions": {
    "input": "denied",
    "screen": "granted"
  },
  "platform": "macos",
  "semanticActions": [],
  "uiTree": true,
  "version": 2
}
```

| height | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":2160} |

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `1` |  |
| sessionId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |
| sequence | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| timestamp | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| width | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":3840} |
| height | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":2160} |
| codec | yes | ["jpeg","h264"] |  |
| keyframe | no | boolean |  |
| videoCodec | no | string | {"pattern":"^avc1\\.[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$"} |
| scale | no | number | {"exclusiveMinimum":0} |
| captureGeneration | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| bytes | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":8388608} |

Example:

```json
{
  "bytes": 2,
  "codec": "jpeg",
  "height": 5,
  "sequence": 8,
  "sessionId": "Uun6hH3WiHohJVyrD-D7JzlGzskmYRQJ_SZoyv78ZxEvBhzSjTrg8ODuxi",
  "timestamp": 5,
  "version": 1,
  "videoCodec": "avc1.fDDe94",
  "width": 4
}
```

## ScreenMeasurementAction

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenMeasurementAction.json), input validation.

### pointer.click

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"pointer.click"` |  |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| button | no | ["left","right"] | {"default":"left"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "kind": "pointer.click",
  "x": 6,
  "y": 6
}
```

### pointer.drag

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"pointer.drag"` |  |
| durationMs | no | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":10000} |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| toX | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| toY | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| button | no | ["left","right"] | {"default":"left"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "durationMs": 1,
  "kind": "pointer.drag",
  "toX": 5,
  "toY": 8,
  "x": 6,
  "y": 2
}
```

### key.press

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"key.press"` |  |
| key | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":32} |
| modifiers | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":4,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["command","shift","option","control","alt","meta","super"]}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "key": "example",
  "kind": "key.press",
  "modifiers": []
}
```

### text.type

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"text.type"` |  |
| text | yes | string | {"maxLength":4096} |

Example:

```json
{
  "kind": "text.type",
  "text": "example"
}
```

### scroll

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"scroll"` |  |
| x | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| y | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| dx | yes | integer | {"minimum":-1000,"maximum":1000} |
| dy | yes | integer | {"minimum":-1000,"maximum":1000} |

Example:

```json
{
  "dx": 2,
  "dy": 6,
  "kind": "scroll",
  "x": 4
}
```

### text.paste

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"text.paste"` |  |
| text | yes | string | {"maxLength":4096} |

Example:

```json
{
  "kind": "text.paste",
  "text": "example"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenMode.json), input validation.

Type: ["background","foreground"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"background"
```

## ScreenServerMessage

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenServerMessage.json), input validation.

### screen.enabled

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"screen.enabled"` |  |
| enabled | yes | boolean |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "enabled": true,
  "type": "screen.enabled"
}
```

### screen.state

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"screen.state"` |  |
| state | yes | [ScreenState.json](schema/ScreenState.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "state": {
    "controller": "human",
    "holder": {
      "agentId": "example",
      "threadId": "example"
    },
    "indicator": false,
    "lifecycle": "stopping",
    "permissions": {
      "accessibility": false,
      "screenRecording": false
    },
    "secureInputAllowed": true,
    "sessionId": "FlPMR8SZpo9_AqJ1KCa0eVSQLiY2qh8diEzUPLs3tsJhH7H6IdhN2-mBqqSM_",
    "target": {
      "bundleIds": [
        "example"
      ],
      "displayId": 9,
      "kind": "display"
    }
  },
  "type": "screen.state"
}
```

### screen.result

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"screen.result"` |  |
| requestId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |
| ok | yes | boolean |  |
| data | no | any JSON value |  |
| error | no | string |  |
| errorCode | no | ["forbidden","permission_denied","approval_required","screen_disabled","denied","read_only","target_gone","not_supported","bounds","busy","timeout","internal","target_busy","foreground_required","focus_changed","window_minimized","window_offscreen","secure_input_required","clipboard_changed"] |  |
| holder | no | object | {"properties":{"sessionId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ScreenId.json"},"owner":{"type":"string","maxLength":1024}},"required":["sessionId","owner"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "data": null,
  "holder": {
    "owner": "example",
    "sessionId": "3KFUkzxC"
  },
  "ok": true,
  "requestId": "8",
  "type": "screen.result"
}
```

## ScreenState

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenState.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| sessionId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |
| lifecycle | yes | ["starting","live","stopping","stopped","failed"] |  |
| controller | yes | ["agent","human","none"] |  |
| mode | no | [ScreenMode.json](schema/ScreenMode.json) | {"default":"background"} |
| secureInputAllowed | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| holder | no | [ScreenAgentScope.json](schema/ScreenAgentScope.json) |  |
| indicator | yes | boolean |  |
| target | yes | [ScreenTarget.json](schema/ScreenTarget.json) |  |
| permissions | yes | [ScreenPermissions.json](schema/ScreenPermissions.json) |  |
| error | no | string | {"maxLength":1024} |
| capabilities | no | [ScreenCapabilities.json](schema/ScreenCapabilities.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "controller": "agent",
  "indicator": false,
  "lifecycle": "stopping",
  "permissions": {
    "accessibility": true,
    "screenRecording": true
  },
  "sessionId": "I7y2OZHFyJASJlyJ3YRiQ7QnBFmRL0dkHnkpTU9pr_5IB",
  "target": {
    "bundleId": "example",
    "kind": "app"
  }
}
```

## SearchFilters

[JSON Schema](schema/SearchFilters.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: after must be <= before when both timestamps are present.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| workspaceId | no | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| after | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| before | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| status | no | [SearchState.json](schema/SearchState.json) |  |
| agentId | no | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| kind | no | [SearchKind.json](schema/SearchKind.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": 8,
  "agentId": "example",
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

## ThreadDetails

[JSON Schema](schema/ThreadDetails.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| workspaceChange | no | object | {"properties":{"commandId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256},"state":{"type":"string","enum":["preparing","applied","failed"]},"at":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Timestamp.json"},"error":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"lossy":{"default":true,"type":"boolean"},"uncertain":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["commandId","state","at"]} |
| workspace | no | object | {"properties":{"id":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"name":{"type":"string","maxLength":256},"path":{"type":"string","maxLength":4096}},"required":["id","name","path"]} |
| mode | no | ["local","worktree"] |  |
| worktree | no | string | {"maxLength":4096} |
| branch | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","maxLength":1024},{"type":"null"}]} |
| head | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","pattern":"^[a-f0-9]{40,64}$"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| ahead | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| behind | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| baseBranch | no | string | {"maxLength":1024} |
| repository | no | [ForgeRepository.json](schema/ForgeRepository.json) |  |
| linkedPr | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"object","properties":{"number":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"state":{"type":"string","enum":["open","closed","merged"]},"url":{"type":"string","format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":4096,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."}},"required":["number","state"]},{"type":"null"}]} |
| machine | no | object | {"properties":{"host":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256},"name":{"type":"string","maxLength":256}},"required":["host","name"]} |
| diff | no | object | {"properties":{"files":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"additions":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"deletions":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["files","additions","deletions"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "ahead": 8,
  "behind": 6,
  "linkedPr": {
    "number": 9,
    "state": "merged",
    "url": "https://example.invalid/"
  },
  "machine": {
    "host": "example",
    "name": "example"
  },
  "mode": "local",
  "worktree": "example"
}
```

## ThreadListEntry

[JSON Schema](schema/ThreadListEntry.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| pinned | no | boolean |  |
| pinOrder | no | number | {"description":"Place among pinned threads, higher first. Set on pin (the daemon's clock when the command names none, so a new pin leads) and cleared on unpin."} |
| unread | no | boolean |  |
| readAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| settledAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| settledReason | no | ["manual","inactivity","pr_merged","pr_closed"] |  |
| snoozedUntil | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| deletedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| activityAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| autoSettleAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| deck | no | [DeckOwnership.json](schema/DeckOwnership.json) |  |
| details | no | [ThreadDetails.json](schema/ThreadDetails.json) |  |
| live | no | [ThreadRunMetadata.json](schema/ThreadRunMetadata.json) |  |
| id | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| workspaceId | yes | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| title | yes | string |  |
| titleSource | no | ["provisional","provider","person","agent"] |  |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| backend | no | ["acp","cursor-sdk"] |  |
| capabilities | no | [Capabilities.json](schema/Capabilities.json) |  |
| permission | no | [PermissionState.json](schema/PermissionState.json) |  |
| handoff | no | object | {"properties":{"sourceThreadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"truncated":{"type":"boolean"},"bytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["sourceThreadId","truncated","bytes"]} |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| effectiveCapabilities | no | [Capabilities.json](schema/Capabilities.json) |  |
| acpSupport | no | [AcpSessionSupport.json](schema/AcpSessionSupport.json) |  |
| status | yes | [ThreadStatus.json](schema/ThreadStatus.json) |  |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| updatedAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| archivedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| imported | no | [ImportedProvenance.json](schema/ImportedProvenance.json) |  |
| lineage | no | [ThreadLineage.json](schema/ThreadLineage.json) |  |
| execution | no | [ExecutionSelection.json](schema/ExecutionSelection.json) |  |
| switch | no | [ThreadSwitch.json](schema/ThreadSwitch.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "archivedAt": 6,
  "autoSettleAt": 9,
  "backend": "acp",
  "createdAt": 6,
  "deletedAt": 3,
  "effectiveCapabilities": {
    "backgroundTaskControl": false,
    "backgroundVisibility": "partial",
    "fork": true,
    "imageInput": false,
    "interruptCascades": false,
    "launchOptions": [],
    "planMode": false,
    "resume": true,
    "rewindFiles": false,
    "steer": true,
    "steeringMode": "queue",
    "subagentTranscripts": false,
    "tokenUsage": false
  },
  "id": "example",
  "pinOrder": 4,
  "provider": "antigravity",
  "status": {
    "state": "failed"
  },
  "title": "example",
  "titleSource": "person",
  "unread": true,
  "updatedAt": 4,
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

## UsageQuery

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageQuery.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: to >= from, their difference is <= 365 days, and groupBy entries are unique.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| from | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| to | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| groupBy | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":7,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"}} |
| filters | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"},"additionalProperties":{"minItems":1,"maxItems":50,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":8192}}} |
| agentTree | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":1000} |
| orderBy | no | ["tokens","cost"] | {"default":"tokens"} |
| equivalentApiCost | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| quotaAccount | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

Example:

```json
{
  "from": "2026-10-01",
  "to": "2026-10-02"
}
```
